event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useMemo, useCallback, MouseEvent, FormEvent } from 'react';\nimport {\n  ChevronLeft,\n  ChevronRight,\n  Shuffle,\n  Plus,\n  RotateCcw,\n  CheckCircle2,\n  XCircle,\n  X\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordCard, CardFilter } from './types';\nimport { initialCards } from './data/initialCards';\nimport CardView from './components/CardView';\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'flashcards_app_data';\n\nconst getStoredCards = (): WordCard[] => {\n  try {\n    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n    if (saved) {\n      const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);\n      if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {\n        return parsed;\n      }\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Failed to read from localStorage:', error);\n  }\n  return initialCards;\n};\n\nexport default function App() {\n  const [cards, setCards] = useState<WordCard[]>(getStoredCards);\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<CardFilter>('all');\n  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // 新規追加フォーム状態\n  const [newWord, setNewWord] = useState('');\n  const [newPhonetic, setNewPhonetic] = useState('');\n  const [newPartOfSpeech, setNewPartOfSpeech] = useState('noun');\n  const [newMeaning, setNewMeaning] = useState('');\n  const [newExampleEn, setNewExampleEn] = useState('');\n  const [newExampleJa, setNewExampleJa] = useState('');\n  const [newCategory, setNewCategory] = useState('日常・会話');\n\n  // カードデータ更新ヘルパー\n  const updateCards = (newCards: WordCard[]) => {\n    setCards(newCards);\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(newCards));\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('Failed to save to localStorage:', e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  // フィルタリング\n  const filteredCards = useMemo(() => {\n    switch (filter) {\n      case 'learning':\n        return cards.filter((card) => !card.isMastered);\n      case 'mastered':\n        return cards.filter((card) => card.isMastered);\n      case 'all':\n      default:\n        return cards;\n    }\n  }, [cards, filter]);\n\n  // 現在のインデックス安全化\n  const activeIndex = filteredCards.length > 0 ? Math.min(currentIndex, filteredCards.length - 1) : 0;\n  const currentCard = filteredCards[activeIndex] || null;\n\n  // 進捗率計算\n  const masteredCount = useMemo(() => cards.filter((c) => c.isMastered).length, [cards]);\n  const progressPercent = cards.length > 0 ? Math.round((masteredCount / cards.length) * 100) : 0;\n\n  // カードフリップ切り替え\n  const handleFlip = useCallback(() => {\n    setIsFlipped((prev) => !prev);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 音声読み上げ\n  const handleSpeak = useCallback((e: MouseEvent, text: string) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {\n      return;\n    }\n    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();\n    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);\n    utterance.lang = 'en-US';\n    utterance.rate = 0.9;\n    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 次のカードへ\n  const handleNext = () => {\n    if (filteredCards.length === 0) return;\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex((prev) => (prev + 1 < filteredCards.length ? prev + 1 : 0));\n  };\n\n  // 前のカードへ\n  const handlePrev = () => {\n    if (filteredCards.length === 0) return;\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex((prev) => (prev - 1 >= 0 ? prev - 1 : filteredCards.length - 1));\n  };\n\n  // カードシャッフル\n  const handleShuffle = () => {\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    const shuffled = [...cards].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);\n    updateCards(shuffled);\n    setCurrentIndex(0);\n  };\n\n  // 覚えた（マスター）処理\n  const handleMasterCard = () => {\n    if (!currentCard) return;\n    const updated = cards.map((c) =>\n      c.id === currentCard.id ? { ...c, isMastered: true } : c\n    );\n    updateCards(updated);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    if (activeIndex >= filteredCards.length - 1 && activeIndex > 0) {\n      setCurrentIndex(activeIndex - 1);\n    }\n  };\n\n  // もう一度（未習得）処理\n data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/CardView.tsx","content":"import { FC, useState, useEffect, MouseEvent, KeyboardEvent } from 'react';\nimport { Volume2, Volume1, RotateCw } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordCard } from '../types';\n\ninterface CardViewProps {\n  card: WordCard;\n  isFlipped: boolean;\n  onFlip: () => void;\n  onSpeak: (e: MouseEvent, text: string) => void;\n}\n\nconst CardView: FC<CardViewProps> = ({ card, isFlipped, onFlip, onSpeak }) => {\n  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setIsPlaying(false);\n  }, [card.id, isFlipped]);\n\n  const handlePlaySound = (e: MouseEvent | KeyboardEvent, text: string) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    setIsPlaying(true);\n    onSpeak(e as unknown as MouseEvent, text);\n\n    const checkSpeaking = () => {\n      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window && window.speechSynthesis.speaking) {\n        setTimeout(checkSpeaking, 100);\n      } else {\n        setIsPlaying(false);\n      }\n    };\n    setTimeout(checkSpeaking, 150);\n  };\n\n  const handleContainerKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (e.target !== e.currentTarget) {\n      return;\n    }\n    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      onFlip();\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"card-container\"\n      onClick={onFlip}\n      role=\"region\"\n      aria-label={`${card.word} のカード。クリックまたはスペースキーで裏返します。`}\n      tabIndex={0}\n      onKeyDown={handleContainerKeyDown}\n    >\n      <div className=\"flashcard\">\n        <div className={`flashcard-inner ${isFlipped ? 'flashcard-flipped' : ''}`}>\n          {/* カード表面 */}\n          <div className=\"card-face card-front\">\n            <span className=\"card-badge\">\n              {card.partOfSpeech} • {card.category}\n            </span>\n\n            <div>\n              <h2 className=\"card-word\">{card.word}</h2>\n              <p className=\"card-phonetic\">{card.phonetic}</p>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className=\"sound-btn\"\n                onClick={(e) => handlePlaySound(e, card.word)}\n                onKeyDown={(e) => {\n                  e.stopPropagation();\n                  if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {\n                    e.preventDefault();\n                    handlePlaySound(e, card.word);\n                  }\n                }}\n                aria-label={`${card.word} の発音を再生`}\n                style={isPlaying ? { background: '#c7d2fe' } : undefined}\n              >\n                {isPlaying ? <Volume1 size={16} /> : <Volume2 size={16} />}\n                <span>{isPlaying ? '再生中...' : '発音を聞く'}</span>\n              </button>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"card-hint\">\n              <RotateCw size={14} />\n              <span>タップして日本語訳を表示</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* カード裏面 */}\n          <div className=\"card-face card-back\">\n            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', width: '100%' }}>\n              <span className=\"card-badge\">\n                {card.partOfSpeech} • {card.category}\n              </span>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className=\"sound-btn\"\n                style={{ margin: 0, padding: '4px 10px', fontSize: '0.75rem', ...(isPlaying ? { background: '#c7d2fe' } : {}) }}\n                onClick={(e) => handlePlaySound(e, card.word)}\n                onKeyDown={(e) => {\n                  e.stopPropagation();\n                  if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {\n                    e.preventDefault();\n                    handlePlaySound(e, card.word);\n                  }\n                }}\n                aria-label={`${card.word} の発音を再生`}\n              >\n                {isPlaying ? <Volume1 size={14} /> : <Volume2 size={14} />}\n                <span>{isPlaying ? '再生中...' : '発音'}</span>\n              </button>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"card-meaning\">{card.meaning}</div>\n\n            <div className=\"card-example\">\n              <p className=\"card-example-en\">{card.exampleEn}</p>\n              <p className=\"card-example-ja\">{card.exampleJa}</p>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"card-hint\">\n              <RotateCw size={14} />\n              <span>タップして表に戻る</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default CardView;","language":"tsx"}

フィルタリング\n  const filteredCards = useMemo(() => {\n    switch (filter) {\n      case 'learning':\n        return cards.filter((card) => !card.isMastered);\n      case 'mastered':\n        return } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordCard } from '../types';\n\ninterface CardViewProps {\n  card: WordCard;\n  isFlipped: boolean;\n  onFlip: () => void;\n  onSpeak: (e: MouseEvent, text: string) => void;\n}\n\nconst CardView: FC<CardViewProps> = ({ card, isFlipped, onFlip, onSpeak }) => {\n  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setIsPlaying(false);\n  }, [card.id, isFlipped]);\n\n  const handlePlaySound = (e: MouseEvent | KeyboardEvent, text: string) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    setIsPlaying(true);\n    onSpeak(e as unknown as MouseEvent, text);\n\n    const checkSpeaking = () => {\n      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window && window.speechSynthesis.speaking) {\n        setTimeout(checkSpeaking, 100);\n      } else {\n        setIsPlaying(false);\n      }\n    };\n    setTimeout(checkSpeaking, 150);\n  };\n\n  const handleContainerKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    if (e.target !== e.currentTarget) {\n      return;\n    }\n    if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      onFlip();\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"card-container\"\n      onClick={onFlip}\n      role=\"region\"\n      aria-label={`${card.word} のカード。クリックまたはスペースキーで裏返します。`}\n      tabIndex={0}\n      onKeyDown={handleContainerKeyDown}\n    >\n      <div className=\"flashcard\">\n        <div className={`flashcard-inner ${isFlipped ? 'flashcard-flipped' : ''}`}>\n          {/* カード表面 */}\n          <div className=\"card-face card-front\">\n            <span className=\"card-badge\">\n              {card.partOfSpeech} • {card.category}\n            </span>\n\n            <div>\n              <h2 className=\"card-word\">{card.word}</h2>\n              <p className=\"card-phonetic\">{card.phonetic}</p>\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className=\"sound-btn\"\n                onClick={(e) => handlePlaySound(e, card.word)}\n                onKeyDown={(e) => {\n                  e.stopPropagation();\n                  if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {\n                    e.preventDefault();\n                    handlePlaySound(e, card.word);\n                  }\n                }}\n                aria-label={`${card.word} の発音を再生`}\n                style={isPlaying ? { background: '#c7d2fe' } : undefined}\n              >\n                {isPlaying ? <Volume1 size={16} /> : <Volume2 size={16} />}\n                <span>{isPlaying ? Plus,\n  RotateCcw,\n  CheckCircle2,\n  XCircle,\n  X\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport { WordCard, CardFilter } from './types';\nimport { initialCards } from './data/initialCards';\nimport CardView useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // 新規追加フォーム状態\n  const [newWord, setNewWord] = useState('');\n  const [newPhonetic, setNewPhonetic] = [newExampleJa, setNewExampleJa] = useState('');\n  const [newCategory, setNewCategory] = useState('日常・会話');\n\n  // カードデータ更新ヘルパー\n  const updateCards = (newCards: WordCard[]) => {\n    setCards(newCards);\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(newCards));\n    } catch (e) {\n      console.error('Failed to save to localStorage:', e);\n    }\n  };\n\n  // フィルタリング\n  const filteredCards = useMemo(() => {\n    switch (filter) {\n      case 'learning':\n        return cards.filter((card) => !card.isMastered);\n      case 'mastered':\n        return cards.filter((card) => card.isMastered);\n      case 'all':\n      default:\n        return cards;\n    }\n  }, [cards, filter]);\n\n  // 現在のインデックス安全化\n  const activeIndex = filteredCards.length > 0 ? useCallback((e: MouseEvent, text: string) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) {\n      return;\n    }\n    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();\n    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);\n    utterance.lang = 'en-US';\n    utterance.rate = 0.9;\n    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);\n  }, []);\n\n  // 次のカードへ\n  const handleNext = () => {\n    if (filteredCards.length === 0) return;\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    setCurrentIndex((prev) => (prev + 1 < filteredCards.length ? prev + 1 : 0));\n  };\n\n  // 前のカードへ\n  const handlePrev = () => {\n const handleMasterCard = () => {\n    if (!currentCard) return;\n    const updated = cards.map((c) =>\n      c.id === currentCard.id ? { ...c, isMastered: true } : c\n    );\n const handleReviewCard = () => {\n    if (!currentCard) return;\n    const updated = cards.map((c) =>\n      c.id === currentCard.id ? { ...c, isMastered: false } : c\n    );\n    updateCards(updated);\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n    handleNext();\n  };\n\n  // 全進捗リセット\n  const handleResetProgress = () => {\n    const reset = cards.map((c) => ({ ...c, isMastered: false }));\n    setIsFlipped(false);\n\n    // 入力リセット\n    setNewWord('');\n    setNewPhonetic('');\n    setNewPartOfSpeech('noun');\n    setNewMeaning('');\n    setNewExampleEn('');\n    setNewExampleJa('');\n    setNewCategory('日常・会話');\n    setIsModalOpen(false);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-wrapper\">\n      {/* ヘッダー */}\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <h1 </div>\n        <div className=\"progress-bar-bg\">\n          <div\n            className=\"progress-bar-fill\"\n            style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}\n          />\n        </div>\n
